## New Harmonized Value-added Tax System Regulations, No. 2 {#new-harmonized-value-added-tax-system-regulations-no.-2 .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2010-151
:::

[EXCISE TAX ACT](/eng/acts/E-15)

Registration 2010-06-17

New Harmonized Value-added Tax System Regulations, No. 2

P.C. 2010-790 2010-06-17
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Finance, pursuant to sections 236.01[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e){#footnotea_e-ID0EKEAA}, 277[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#footnoteb_e){#footnoteb_e-ID0EIEAA} and
277.1[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#footnotec_e){#footnotec_e-ID0EGEAA} of the [Excise
Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^d^](#footnoted_e){#footnoted_e-ID0EDEAA}, hereby makes
the annexed New Harmonized Value-added Tax System Regulations, No. 2.

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-758179 .Part}

\[

- SOR/2019-59, s. 26(F)

\]

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Excise Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/E-15). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[imported taxable supply]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[imported taxable supply]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in section 217 of the Act. ([fourniture taxable
      importée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[provincial investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provincial investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for a
      particular province at any time means an investment plan

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is at that time a financial institution
        described in section 11 of the [Selected Listed Financial
        Institutions Attribution Method (GST/HST)
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171), the units of which
        are permitted, under the laws of Canada or a province, to be
        sold only in the particular province;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is at that time a stratified investment
        plan, all the series of which are provincial series for the
        particular province; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that meets the following criteria:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the investment plan has, throughout the
          taxation year in which the fiscal year of the investment plan
          that includes that time ends, a permanent establishment in the
          particular province, as determined in accordance with section
          3 of the [Selected Listed Financial Institutions Attribution
          Method (GST/HST) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171),
          and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the investment plan does not have,
          throughout that taxation year, a permanent establishment in a
          province other than the particular province, as determined in
          accordance with section 3 of the [Selected Listed Financial
          Institutions Attribution Method (GST/HST)
          Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171). ([régime de
          placement provincial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[provincial series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provincial series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a financial
      institution for a particular province means a provincial series of
      the financial institution, as defined in subsection 1(1) of the
      [Selected Listed Financial Institutions Attribution Method
      (GST/HST) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171), the units
      of which are permitted, under the laws of Canada or a province, to
      be sold in the particular province. ([série
      provinciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[provincial stratified investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provincial stratified investment
      plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a stratified investment plan
      (other than a provincial investment plan) with one or more
      provincial series. ([régime de placement stratifié
      provincial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 1(1) of the [Selected Listed Financial Institutions
      Attribution Method (GST/HST)
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171). ([série]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[stratified investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[stratified investment plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 1(1) of the [Selected Listed Financial
      Institutions Attribution Method (GST/HST)
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171). ([régime de placement
      stratifié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in subsection
      1(1) of the [Selected Listed Financial Institutions Attribution
      Method (GST/HST)
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171). ([unité]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of definitions to
  adaptations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the definitions in this
  section apply in subsections 218.1(1) and (1.2), 220.07(1) to (4) and
  220.08(1) of the Act as adapted by these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 4
- [SOR/2019-59, s. 27](#1115737-1117276){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115737-1117276.html"}
:::

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Permanent Establishment in a Province]{.HTitleText1} {#h-758189 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed classes

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of subsection 132.1(3) of the Act, the following classes of
  persons are prescribed:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} charities;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} non-profit organizations; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} selected public service bodies, as defined in
    subsection 259(1) of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permanent establishment in a province

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 132.1(3) of the Act, a
  person referred to in subsection (1) is deemed to have a permanent
  establishment in a province if a place in the province would be a
  permanent establishment (as defined for the purposes of Part IV of the
  [Income Tax Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._945)) of the
  person under the following circumstances:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person were a corporation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person's activities were a business for the
    purposes of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2019-59, s. 28\]]{.Repealed}

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2019-59, s. 28\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2011-56, s. 25
- SOR/2013-71, s. 5
- [SOR/2019-59, s. 28](#1115744-1117279){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115744-1117279.html"}
:::

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Travel and Other Allowances]{.HTitleText1} {#h-758206 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Percentage

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subparagraph (i) of the description of B in paragraph 174(f) of the Act,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if all or substantially all of the supplies for which
  the allowance is paid were made in a particular participating province
  or if the allowance is paid for the use of the motor vehicle and all
  or substantially all of that use is in a particular participating
  province, the percentage is the total of the rate set out in
  subsection 165(1) of the Act and the tax rate for that particular
  participating province; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (a) applies, if all or substantially
  all of the supplies for which the allowance is paid were made in two
  or more participating provinces or if the allowance is paid for the
  use of the motor vehicle and all or substantially all of that use is
  in two or more participating provinces, the percentage is the total of
  the rate set out in subsection 165(1) of the Act and the lowest tax
  rate among the tax rates for those participating provinces.

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Direct Sellers]{.HTitleText1} {#h-758211 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment for participating provinces

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of the new
harmonized value-added tax system, subsections 178.3(5) and (6) and
178.4(5) and (6) of the Act do not apply in respect of supplies of
exclusive products made by independent sales contractors.

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[Non-Exclusive Use of Passenger Vehicle or Aircraft]{.HTitleText1} {#h-758214 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bringing between participating provinces

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of the
description of I in subparagraph (iv) of the description of A in
paragraph 202(4)(b) of the Act, the rate is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the bringing into a participating
  province of a vehicle or aircraft from another participating province,
  the percentage determined by the formula

  ::: {#758218 .Paragraph}
  A - B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the tax rate for the participating province into which the
      vehicle or aircraft is brought, and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the tax rate for the participating province from which the
      vehicle or aircraft is removed; and
  :::

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, 0%.

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[Property and Services Brought into a Province]{.HTitleText1} {#h-758223 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758224 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** The following definitions
apply in this Part.

[[provincial rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[provincial rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for or in relation to a
    province, means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a participating province, the tax
      rate for that province; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a non-participating province,
      0%. ([taux provincial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[specified item]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specified item]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a province,
    means property or a service that is an item included in a schedule
    to the [Deduction for Provincial Rebate (GST/HST)
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-65) and in respect of which
    an amount may be paid or credited under an Act of the legislature of
    the province. ([article déterminé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

### [DIVISION 1.1]{.HLabel2}[Tax on Importation of Goods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758231 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed goods --- paragraph
212.1(2)(a)

**[[6.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 212.1(2)(a) of the Act, goods the value of which is determined
for the purposes of Division III of Part IX of the Act under section 15
of the [Value of Imported Goods (GST/HST)
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-91-30) are prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 19, s. 51
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Tax on Imported Taxable Supplies]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758236 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed extent --- paragraph
218.1(1)(a) of Act

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 218.1(1)(a) of the Act, the prescribed extent is an extent of
at least 10%.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 6
- [SOR/2019-59, s. 29](#1115745-1117282){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115745-1117282.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation --- subsection 218.1(1) of Act

**[[7.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.01 .sectionLabel}** If a person is the
recipient of an imported taxable supply of property or a service and the
person is a provincial investment plan or provincial stratified
investment plan at the time an amount of consideration for the supply
becomes due or is paid without having become due, subsection 218.1(1) of
the Act is adapted in respect of the amount of consideration for the
supply as follows:

> ::: ReadAsText
> - **218.1** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part,
>
>   - [(a)]{.lawlabel} every person that is the recipient of an imported
>     taxable supply of intangible personal property or a service and
>     that is a provincial investment plan or provincial stratified
>     investment plan at the time an amount of consideration for the
>     supply becomes due or is paid without having become due must, for
>     that amount of consideration and for each participating province,
>     pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada, in addition to the tax
>     imposed by section 218, tax equal to the amount determined by the
>     formula
>
>     ::: {#1117289 .Paragraph}
>     A × B × C
>
>     where
>
>     [A]{.dfn}
>     :   is the tax rate for the participating province,
>
>     [B]{.dfn}
>     :   is the value of that consideration that is paid or becomes due
>         at that time, and
>
>     [C]{.dfn}
>     :   is
>         - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial stratified
>           investment plan, the total of all amounts, each of which is
>           the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the property
>           or service was acquired for consumption, use or supply in
>           the course of activities relating to a provincial series of
>           the person for the participating province, as determined in
>           accordance with section 51 of the [Selected Listed Financial
>           Institutions Attribution Method (GST/HST)
>           Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171),
>
>         - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial investment
>           plan for the participating province, 100%, and
>
>         - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial investment
>           plan for a province other than the participating province,
>           0%; and
>     :::
>
>   - [(b)]{.lawlabel} every person
>
>     - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is a registrant and is the recipient of a
>       supply of property included in paragraph (b) of the definition
>       [[imported taxable supply]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section
>       217, or
>
>     - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that is the recipient of a supply of property
>       included in any of paragraphs (b.01) to (b.3) or (c.1) to (e) of
>       the definition [[imported taxable supply]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
>       in section 217
>
>     and that is a provincial investment plan or a provincial
>     stratified investment plan at the time an amount of consideration
>     for the supply becomes due or is paid without having become due
>     shall, for that amount of consideration and for each participating
>     province, pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada, in addition to
>     the tax imposed by section 218, tax equal to the amount determined
>     by the formula
>
>     ::: {#1117301 .Paragraph}
>     A × B × C
>
>     where
>
>     [A]{.dfn}
>     :   is the tax rate for the participating province,
>
>     [B]{.dfn}
>     :   is the value of that consideration that is paid or becomes due
>         at that time, and
>
>     [C]{.dfn}
>     :   is
>         - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial stratified
>           investment plan, the total of all amounts, each of which is
>           the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the property
>           was acquired for consumption, use or supply in the course of
>           activities relating to a provincial series of the person for
>           the participating province, as determined in accordance with
>           section 51 of the [Selected Listed Financial Institutions
>           Attribution Method (GST/HST)
>           Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171),
>
>         - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial investment
>           plan for the participating province, 100%, and
>
>         - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial investment
>           plan for a province other than the participating province,
>           0%.
>     :::
> :::

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 6

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- paragraph 218.1(1)(a)
of Act

**[[7.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.02 .sectionLabel}** Paragraph 218.1(1)(a)
of the Act does not apply in respect of an amount of consideration for
an imported taxable supply of intangible personal property or a service
made to a person if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is a provincial stratified investment plan
  at the time the amount of consideration becomes due or is paid without
  having become due; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the following formula,
  expressed as a percentage, is less than 10%:

  ::: {#1117314 .Paragraph}
  A/B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is the extent to which
      the property or service is acquired for consumption, use or supply
      in the course of activities relating to a provincial series of the
      person for a participating province, as determined in accordance
      with section 51 of the [Selected Listed Financial Institutions
      Attribution Method (GST/HST)
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171), and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is the extent to which
      the property or service is acquired for consumption, use or supply
      in the course of activities relating to a provincial series of the
      person for any province, as determined in accordance with section
      51 of the [Selected Listed Financial Institutions Attribution
      Method (GST/HST) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171).
  :::

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 6
- [SOR/2019-59, s. 29](#1115745-1117318){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115745-1117318.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7.02-20130418.html "Link to previous version of section 7.02"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation --- subsection 218.1(1.2) of
Act

**[[7.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.03 .sectionLabel}** If a person is a
qualifying taxpayer, within the meaning of subsection 217.1(1) of the
Act, for a [[specified year]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} of the person, as
defined in section 217 of the Act, and if the person is a provincial
investment plan or provincial stratified investment plan at any time in
a fiscal year of the person that ends in the specified year, subsection
218.1(1.2) of the Act is adapted in respect of the specified year as
follows:

> ::: ReadAsText
> - [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, every qualifying taxpayer
>   that is a provincial investment plan or provincial stratified
>   investment plan at any time in a fiscal year of the qualifying
>   taxpayer that ends in a specified year of the qualifying taxpayer
>   shall, for the specified year and for each particular participating
>   province, pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada, in addition to the
>   tax payable under section 218.01, tax calculated at the tax rate for
>   the particular participating province on
>
>   - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if an election under subsection 217.2(1) is in
>     effect for the specified year, the amount determined by the
>     formula
>
>     ::: {#1117324 .Paragraph}
>     A + B
>
>     where
>
>     [A]{.dfn}
>     :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is an amount in
>         respect of an internal charge for the specified year that is
>         greater than zero determined by the formula
>         ::: {#1117327 .NestedFormula}
>         A~1~ × A~2~
>
>         where
>
>         [A~1~]{.dfn}
>         :   is the internal charge, and
>
>         [A~2~]{.dfn}
>         :   is
>             - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial stratified
>               investment plan, the total of all amounts, each of which
>               is the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the
>               internal charge is attributable to outlays or expenses
>               that were made or incurred to consume, use or supply the
>               whole or part of property or of a qualifying service in
>               respect of which the internal charge is attributable, in
>               carrying on, engaging in or conducting an activity of
>               the qualifying taxpayer relating to a provincial series
>               of the qualifying taxpayer for the particular
>               participating province, as determined in accordance with
>               section 51 of the [Selected Listed Financial
>               Institutions Attribution Method (GST/HST)
>               Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171),
>
>             - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial investment
>               plan for the particular participating province, 100%,
>               and
>
>             - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial
>               investment plan for a province other than the particular
>               participating province, 0%, and
>         :::
>
>     [B]{.dfn}
>     :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is an amount in
>         respect of an external charge for the specified year that is
>         greater than zero determined by the formula
>         ::: {#1117335 .NestedFormula}
>         B~1~ × B~2~
>
>         where
>
>         [B~1~]{.dfn}
>         :   is the external charge, and
>
>         [B~2~]{.dfn}
>         :   is
>             - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial stratified
>               investment plan, the total of all amounts, each of which
>               is the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the
>               whole or part of the outlay or expense, which
>               corresponds to the external charge, was made or incurred
>               to consume, use or supply the whole or part of property
>               or of a qualifying service in respect of which the
>               external charge is attributable, in carrying on,
>               engaging in or conducting an activity of the qualifying
>               taxpayer relating to a provincial series of the
>               qualifying taxpayer for the particular participating
>               province, as determined in accordance with section 51 of
>               the [Selected Listed Financial Institutions Attribution
>               Method (GST/HST)
>               Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171),
>
>             - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial investment
>               plan for the particular participating province, 100%,
>               and
>
>             - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial
>               investment plan for a province other than the particular
>               participating province, 0%; and
>         :::
>     :::
>
>   - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the total of all amounts, each
>     of which is an amount in respect of qualifying consideration for
>     the specified year that is greater than zero determined by the
>     formula
>
>     ::: {#1117343 .Paragraph}
>     C × D
>
>     where
>
>     [C]{.dfn}
>     :   is the qualifying consideration, and
>
>     [D]{.dfn}
>     :   is
>         - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial stratified
>           investment plan, the total of all amounts, each of which is
>           the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the whole or
>           part of the outlay or expense, which corresponds to the
>           qualifying consideration, was made or incurred to consume,
>           use or supply the whole or part of property or of a
>           qualifying service in respect of which the qualifying
>           consideration is attributable, in carrying on, engaging in
>           or conducting an activity of the qualifying taxpayer
>           relating to a provincial series of the qualifying taxpayer
>           for the particular participating province, as determined in
>           accordance with section 51 of the [Selected Listed Financial
>           Institutions Attribution Method (GST/HST)
>           Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171),
>
>         - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial investment
>           plan for the particular participating province, 100%, and
>
>         - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial investment
>           plan for a province other than the particular participating
>           province, 0%.
>     :::
> :::

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-59, s. 29](#1115745-1117351){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115745-1117351.html"}
:::

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Tax on Property and Services Brought Into a Participating Province]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758265 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional specified item

**[[7.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this Division, property or a service in respect of which a person has
been paid or credited, or is entitled to be paid or credited, a
qualifying amount (as defined in section 1 of the [Credit for Provincial
Relief (HST) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2011-57)) is a specified
item in respect of Ontario.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2011-56, s. 26
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified provincial tax

**[[7.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.2 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of the
new harmonized value-added tax system, paragraph (c) of the definition
[[specified provincial tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in section 220.01 of
the Act is adapted as follows:

> ::: ReadAsText
> - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle registered in the province
>   of Newfoundland and Labrador, the tax imposed under Part VIII of the
>   Revenue Administration Act, S.N.L. 2009, c. R-15.01, as amended from
>   time to time; and
> :::

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2011-56, s. 26
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified provincial tax --- Ontario

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph (d) of the definition [[specified provincial
tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in section 220.01 of the Act, in the case of a
vehicle registered in the province of Ontario, a prescribed tax is the
tax imposed under the Retail Sales Tax Act, R.S.O. 1990, c. R.31, as
amended from time to time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-191, s. 23
:::

[Previous
Version](section-8-20100701.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified provincial tax --- Prince
Edward Island

**[[8.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph (d) of the definition [[specified provincial
tax]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in section 220.01 of the Act, in the case of a
vehicle registered in the province of Prince Edward Island, the
prescribed tax is the tax imposed under the Retail Sales Tax Act,
S.P.E.I. 2012, c. 22, as amended from time to time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-44, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation of tax --- subsection
220.05(1)

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 220.05(1) of the Act, the amount of tax payable under that
subsection by a person that brings, at a particular time, tangible
personal property into a participating province from another province is
determined by the formula

::: {#758287 .Section}
A × B

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the percentage determined by the formula
    ::: {#758290 .NestedFormula}
    C - D

    where

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the tax rate for the participating province, and

    [D]{.dfn}
    :   is
        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the property is a specified item in
          respect of the other province, 0%, and

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the provincial rate for
          the other province; and
    :::

[B]{.dfn}
:   is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the property is a specified motor vehicle that
      the person is required to register under the laws of the
      participating province relating to the registration of motor
      vehicles, the specified value in respect of the specified motor
      vehicle,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the property is not a specified motor vehicle
      referred to in paragraph (a) and consideration was paid or payable
      in respect of a supply of the property made by way of sale at any
      time to the person by another person with which the person dealt
      at arm's length, the lesser of the value of that consideration and
      the fair market value of the property at the particular time, and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the fair market value of the
      property at the particular time.
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-taxable property --- subsection
220.05(3)

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 220.05(3)(b) of the Act, no tax is payable under subsection
220.05(1) of the Act in respect of property brought into a participating
province by a person if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the property is supplied by the person to a recipient
  that paid tax under section 220.06 of the Act in respect of the supply
  and is delivered or made available to the recipient in the
  participating province or sent by mail or courier to an address in the
  participating province;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total of all amounts, each of which is an amount
  of tax that would, in the absence of this paragraph and paragraphs
  11(b) and 15(b), become payable under Division IV.1 of Part IX of the
  Act by the person and in respect of which subsection 220.09(3) of the
  Act would not apply if that tax became payable by the person, is \$25
  or less in the calendar month that includes

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of property that is a specified motor
    vehicle that the person is required to register under the laws of
    the participating province relating to the registration of motor
    vehicles, the day that is the earlier of the day on which the person
    registers the vehicle and the day on or before which the person is
    required to register the vehicle, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the day on which the property
    is brought into the participating province; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the property is not a specified motor vehicle that
  the person is required to register under the laws of the participating
  province relating to the registration of motor vehicles, the person is
  a provincial investment plan or a provincial stratified investment
  plan and, in the case of a provincial stratified investment plan, the
  person is acquiring the property for the purpose of consumption, use
  or supply in the course of activities relating to one or more
  provincial series of the person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-191, s. 24(F)
- [SOR/2019-59, s. 30](#1115747-1117355){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115747-1117355.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-10-20120920.html "Link to previous version of section 10"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-taxable property --- subsection
220.06(3)

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 220.06(3)(b) of the Act, no tax is payable under subsection
220.06(1) of the Act in respect of a supply of property made to a
recipient that is delivered or made available to the recipient in a
participating province, or is sent by mail or courier to an address in
the participating province, if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the supply is made by a person that paid tax under
  section 220.05 or 220.07 of the Act in respect of bringing the
  property into the participating province;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total of all amounts, each of which is an amount
  of tax that would, in the absence of this paragraph and paragraphs
  10(b) and 15(b), become payable under Division IV.1 of Part IX of the
  Act by the recipient and in respect of which subsection 220.09(3) of
  the Act would not apply if that tax became payable by the recipient,
  is \$25 or less in the calendar month that includes the day on which
  the property is delivered or made available to the recipient in the
  participating province; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the recipient is a provincial investment plan or a
  provincial stratified investment plan and, in the case of a provincial
  stratified investment plan, the recipient is acquiring the property
  for the purpose of consumption, use or supply in the course of
  activities relating to one or more provincial series of the recipient.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-191, s. 25(F)

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-taxable property --- restriction

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraphs 220.05(3)(a) and 220.06(3)(a) of the Act, property that is
included in section 18, 20 or 21 of Part I of Schedule X to the Act is
prescribed property, except if the property is included in another

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation --- subsections 220.07(1) to
(4) of Act

**[[12.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.1 .sectionLabel}** If a person imports
goods, other than a specified motor vehicle, into Canada, if the person
is a provincial investment plan or a provincial stratified investment
plan at the time the goods are released and if, in the case of a
provincial stratified investment plan, the goods are imported for the
purpose of consumption, use or supply in the course of activities
relating to one or more provincial series of the person, subsections
220.07(1) to (4) of the Act are adapted in respect of the importation as
follows:

> ::: ReadAsText
> - **220.07** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, every person that
>   imports goods, other than a specified motor vehicle, that are
>   accounted for as [[commercial goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} (as
>   defined in subsection 212.1(1)) under section 32 of the [Customs
>   Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), that is a provincial investment plan or
>   provincial stratified investment plan at the time the goods are
>   released and that is liable under that Act to pay duties on the
>   goods, or would be so liable if they were subject to duty, shall
>   pay, in addition to the tax imposed under section 212, tax to Her
>   Majesty in Right of Canada equal to the total of all amounts, each
>   of which is determined for a participating province by the formula
>
>   ::: {#1117365 .Subsection}
>   A × B × C
>
>   where
>
>   [A]{.dfn}
>   :   is the value of the goods,
>
>   [B]{.dfn}
>   :   is the tax rate for the participating province, and
>
>   [C]{.dfn}
>   :   is
>       - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial stratified
>         investment plan, the total of all amounts, each of which is
>         the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the person
>         acquired the goods for consumption, use or supply in the
>         course of activities relating to a provincial series of the
>         person for the participating province, as determined in
>         accordance with section 51 of the [Selected Listed Financial
>         Institutions Attribution Method (GST/HST)
>         Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171),
>
>       - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial investment plan
>         for the participating province, 100%, and
>
>       - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a provincial investment plan
>         for a province other than the participating province, 0%.
>   :::
>
> - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Tax under subsection (1) does not apply to goods
>   included in Schedule VII.
>
> - [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the value of
>   goods is equal to the value of the goods determined in accordance
>   with section 215.
>
> - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Tax under subsection (1) on goods imported by a
>   person becomes payable by that person on the day on which the goods
>   are released.
> :::

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-59, s. 32](#1115751-1117377){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115751-1117377.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation --- subsection 220.08(1) of
Act

**[[12.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.2 .sectionLabel}** If a person is the
recipient of a taxable supply made in a particular province of property
or a service, if the person is a provincial investment plan or
provincial stratified investment plan at the time an amount of
consideration for the supply becomes due or is paid without having
become due and if, in the case of a provincial stratified investment
plan, the person is acquiring the property or service for consumption,
use or supply in the course of activities relating to one or more
provincial series of the person, subsection 220.08(1) of the Act is
adapted in respect of the amount of consideration for the supply as
follows:

> ::: ReadAsText
> - **220.08** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, every person that
>   is the recipient of a taxable supply made in a particular province
>   of property or a service and that is a provincial investment plan or
>   provincial stratified investment plan at the time an amount of
>   consideration for the supply becomes due or is paid without having
>   become due must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada, in respect of
>   the amount of consideration, tax equal to the amount determined in
>   accordance with section 13 of the New Harmonized Value-added Tax
>   System Regulations, No. 2.
> :::

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-59, s. 32](#1115751-1117384){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115751-1117384.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation of tax --- subsection
220.08(1) of Act

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 220.08(1) of the Act, the amount of tax payable under that
subsection by a recipient of a taxable supply made in a particular
province of property or a service in respect of an amount of
consideration for the supply that becomes due, or is paid without having
become due, at any time is the total of all amounts, each of which is
determined for a participating province by the formula

::: {#758320 .Section}
A × B × C

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the percentage determined by the formula
    ::: {#758323 .NestedFormula}
    D - E

    where

    [D]{.dfn}
    :   is the tax rate for the participating province, and

    [E]{.dfn}
    :   is
        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the property or service is a specified
          item in respect of the particular province, 0%, and

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the provincial rate for
          the particular province;
    :::

[B]{.dfn}
:   is the value of the consideration that is paid or becomes due at
    that time; and

[C]{.dfn}
:   is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the recipient is a stratified investment plan
      with one or more provincial series, the total of all amounts, each
      of which is the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the
      recipient acquired the property or service for consumption, use or
      supply in the course of activities relating to a provincial series
      of the financial institution for the participating province, as
      determined in accordance with section 51 of the [Selected Listed
      Financial Institutions Attribution Method (GST/HST)
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171);

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the recipient is a provincial investment plan
      for the participating province, 100%;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the recipient is a provincial investment plan
      for a province other than the participating province, 0%; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the extent (expressed as a
      percentage) to which the recipient acquired the property or
      service for consumption, use or supply in the participating
      province.
:::

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 7
- [SOR/2019-59, s. 33](#1115753-1117387){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115753-1117387.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-taxable property and services ---
restriction

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 220.08(3)(a) of the Act, a supply that is included in section
4 of Part II of Schedule X to the Act is a prescribed supply, except if
the supply is included in another section of that Part.

::: HistoricalNote

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-taxable property and services ---
subsection 220.08(3)

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 220.08(3)(b) of the Act, no tax is payable under subsection
220.08(1) of the Act in respect of an amount of consideration for a
supply of property or a service made in a particular province to a
person that becomes due, or is paid without having become due, at a
particular time if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is neither a provincial stratified
  investment plan that acquired the property or service for consumption,
  use or supply in the course of activities relating to one or more
  provincial series of the person nor a provincial investment plan and
  the extent to which the person acquired the property or service for
  consumption, use or supply in participating provinces that, at the
  particular time, have a tax rate that is greater than the provincial
  rate for the particular province is less than 10%;

- [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} the person is a provincial stratified investment
  plan that acquired the property or service for consumption, use or
  supply in the course of activities relating to one or more provincial
  series of the person and the extent to which the person acquired the
  property or service for consumption, use or supply in the course of
  activities relating to one or more provincial series of the person for
  participating provinces having a tax rate that is greater than the
  provincial rate for the particular province is less than 10%, where
  that extent (expressed as a percentage) is determined by the formula

  ::: {#758351 .Paragraph}
  A/B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is the extent to which
      the property or service is acquired for consumption, use or supply
      in the course of activities relating to a provincial series of the
      investment plan for a participating province having a tax rate
      that is greater than the provincial rate for the particular
      province, as determined in accordance with section 51 of the
      [Selected Listed Financial Institutions Attribution Method
      (GST/HST) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171), and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is the extent to which
      the property or service is acquired for consumption, use or supply
      in the course of activities relating to a provincial series of the
      investment plan for any province, as determined in accordance with
      section 51 of the [Selected Listed Financial Institutions
      Attribution Method (GST/HST)
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171); or
  :::

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total of all amounts, each of which is an amount
  of tax that would, in the absence of this paragraph and paragraphs
  10(b) and 11(b), become payable under Division IV.1 of Part IX of the
  Act by the person and in respect of which subsection 220.09(3) of the
  Act would not apply if that tax became payable by the person, is \$25
  or less in the calendar month that includes the particular time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2011-56, s. 29(F)
- SOR/2013-71, s. 9
- [SOR/2019-59, s. 35](#1115756-1117391){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1115756-1117391.html"}

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Rebates in Respect of Property and Services Removed from a Participating Province]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758358 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for rebate --- subsection
261.1(1)

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
determining if a rebate is payable under subsection 261.1(1) of the Act
to a person that is the recipient of a supply of property made in a
participating province that removes the property from the participating
province to another province, the following conditions are prescribed:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the property is acquired for consumption, use or
  supply exclusively outside the participating province;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a consumer of the property and the
  property is not a specified motor vehicle, the person is resident in
  the other province; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person pays all provincial levies, if any, that
  are payable by the person in respect of the property and that are
  prescribed for the purposes of section 154 of the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation of rebate --- subsection
261.1(1)

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 261.1(1) of the Act, the amount of a rebate payable under
that subsection to a person that is a recipient of a supply of property
made in a participating province that removes the property from the
participating province to another province is determined by the formula

::: {#758366 .Section}
A - B

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the amount of tax that became payable, and was paid, under
    subsection 165(2) of the Act by the person in respect of the supply;
    and

[B]{.dfn}
:   is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the property is a specified item in respect of
      the other province, zero, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the amount of tax that would
      have become payable under subsection 165(2) of the Act by the
      person in respect of the supply if that tax were calculated at the
      provincial rate for the other province.
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for rebate --- section 261.2

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 261.2 of the Act, a prescribed condition is payment by the
person of all provincial levies, if any, that are payable by the person
in respect of the property and that are prescribed for the purposes of
section 154 of the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation of rebate --- section 261.2

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 261.2 of the Act, the amount of a rebate payable under that
section to the person that imports property for consumption or use
exclusively in a province is determined by the formula

::: {#758376 .Section}
A - B

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the amount of tax paid under subsection 212.1(2) of the Act by
    the person in respect of the property; and

[B]{.dfn}
:   is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the property is a specified item in respect of
      the province, zero, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the amount of tax that would
      have become payable under subsection 212.1(2) of the Act by the
      person in respect of the property if that tax were calculated at
      the provincial rate for the province.
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for rebate --- subsection
261.3(1)

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 261.3(1) of the Act, a rebate is only payable to a person
under that subsection in respect of a supply of intangible personal
property or a service made in a participating province if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an amount of tax becomes payable, at a particular
  time, under subsection 165(2) of the Act by the person in respect of
  the supply calculated on an amount of consideration for the supply;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the extent to which the person acquired the property
  or service for consumption, use or supply elsewhere than in
  participating provinces that, at the particular time, have a tax rate
  that is greater than, or equal to, the tax rate for the participating
  province is 10% or more.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation of rebate --- subsection
261.3(1)

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 261.3(1) of the Act, the amount of a rebate payable under
that subsection to a person that is the recipient of a supply made in a
particular participating province of intangible personal property or a
service is determined by the formula

::: {#758388 .Section}
A - B

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the amount of tax referred to in paragraph 20(a) in respect of
    the supply; and

[B]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all amounts, each of which is determined for a
    participating province by the formula
    ::: {#758392 .NestedFormula}
    C × D

    where

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is
        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the property or service is a specified
          item in respect of the participating province, zero, and

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the amount of tax that, at
          the particular time referred to in paragraph 20(a), would have
          become payable under subsection 165(2) of the Act by the
          person in respect of the supply if that tax were calculated on
          the amount of consideration referred to in that paragraph for
          the supply,

          - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the tax rate for the participating
            province is lower than the tax rate for the particular
            participating province, at the tax rate for the
            participating province, and

          - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, at the tax rate for the
            particular participating province, and

    [D]{.dfn}
    :   is the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the person
        acquired the property or service for consumption, use or supply
        in the participating province.
    :::
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed person --- subsection
261.31(2) of Act

- **[[21.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection
  261.31(2) of the Act, a selected listed financial institution that is
  a provincial stratified investment plan is a prescribed person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed amount --- subsection
  261.31(2) of Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 261.31(2) of the Act,
  the amount of a rebate payable under that subsection to a person where
  tax under subsection 165(2) or section 212.1 or 218.1 of the Act or
  Division IV.1 of Part IX of the Act becomes payable by the person at a
  particular time is equal to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a provincial stratified investment
    plan,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the tax is payable under subsection 165(2) of
      the Act in respect of a supply of property or a service, the total
      of all amounts, each of which is determined for a provincial
      series of the person by the formula

      ::: {#758407 .Subparagraph}
      (A -- B) × C

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the amount of that tax,

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is
          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the provincial series is for a
            participating province, the amount of tax that would have
            become payable under subsection 165(2) of the Act in respect
            of the supply at the particular time if that tax were
            calculated at the tax rate for that province, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, zero, and

      [C]{.dfn}
      :   is the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the
          property or service was acquired for consumption, use or
          supply in the course of the activities relating to the
          provincial series, as determined in accordance with section 51
          of the [Selected Listed Financial Institutions Attribution
          Method (GST/HST) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171),
      :::

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the tax is payable under section 212.1 or
      218.1 or subsection 220.06(1) of the Act in respect of tangible
      personal property, the total of all amounts, each of which is
      determined for a provincial series of the person by the formula

      ::: {#758415 .Subparagraph}
      (D -- E) × F

      where

      [D]{.dfn}
      :   is the amount of that tax,

      [E]{.dfn}
      :   is
          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the provincial series is for a
            participating province, the amount of tax that would have
            become payable under that section or subsection in respect
            of the property at the particular time if that tax were
            calculated at the tax rate for that province, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, zero, and

      [F]{.dfn}
      :   is the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the
          tangible personal property was acquired or imported for
          consumption, use or supply in the course of the activities
          relating to the provincial series, as determined in accordance
          with section 51 of the [Selected Listed Financial Institutions
          Attribution Method (GST/HST)
          Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171),
      :::

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the tax is payable under subsection
      220.05(1) or 220.07(1) of the Act in respect of bringing tangible
      personal property into a particular participating province, the
      total of all amounts, each of which is determined for a provincial
      series of the person by the formula

      ::: {#758423 .Subparagraph}
      (G -- H) × I

      where

      [G]{.dfn}
      :   is the amount of that tax,

      [H]{.dfn}
      :   is
          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the provincial series is for the
            particular participating province, the amount of that tax,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} if the provincial series is for a
            participating province other than the particular
            participating province, the amount of tax that would have
            become payable under that subsection in respect of the
            bringing in of the property if the property were brought
            into the other participating province, and

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, zero, and

      [I]{.dfn}
      :   is the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the
          property was brought into the particular participating
          province for consumption, use or supply in the course of the
          activities relating to the provincial series, as determined in
          accordance with section 51 of the [Selected Listed Financial
          Institutions Attribution Method (GST/HST)
          Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-171), and
      :::

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, zero;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a provincial investment plan,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the tax is payable under subsection 165(2) of
      the Act in respect of a supply of property or a service, the
      amount determined by the formula

      ::: {#758434 .Subparagraph}
      A -- B

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the amount of that tax, and

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is
          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a provincial investment
            plan for a participating province, the amount of tax that
            would have become payable under subsection 165(2) of the Act
            in respect of the supply at the particular time if that tax
            were calculated at the tax rate for that province, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, zero,
      :::

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the tax is payable under section 212.1 or
      218.1 or subsection 220.06(1) of the Act in respect of tangible
      personal property, the amount determined by the formula

      ::: {#758441 .Subparagraph}
      C -- D

      where

      [C]{.dfn}
      :   is the amount of that tax, and

      [D]{.dfn}
      :   is
          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a provincial investment
            plan for a participating province, the amount of tax that
            would have become payable under that section or subsection
            in respect of the property at the particular time if that
            tax were calculated at the tax rate for that province, and

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, zero,
      :::

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the tax is payable under subsection
      220.05(1) or 220.07(1) of the Act in respect of bringing tangible
      personal property into a particular participating province, the
      amount determined by the formula

      ::: {#758448 .Subparagraph}
      E -- F

      where

      [E]{.dfn}
      :   is the amount of that tax, and

      [F]{.dfn}
      :   is
          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a provincial investment
            plan for the particular participating province, the amount
            of that tax,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a provincial investment
            plan for a participating province other than the particular
            participating province, the amount of tax that would have
            become payable under that subsection in respect of the
            bringing in of the property if the property were brought
            into the other participating province, and

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, zero, and
      :::

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, zero; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the tax is payable under subsection 165(2) of
      the Act in respect of a supply of property or a service, the
      amount determined by the formula

      ::: {#758458 .Subparagraph}
      A -- B

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the amount of that tax, and

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is determined for a
          participating province by the formula
          ::: {#758462 .NestedFormula}
          C × D

          where

          [C]{.dfn}
          :   is the amount of tax that would have become payable under
              subsection 165(2) of the Act in respect of the supply at
              the particular time if that tax were calculated at the tax
              rate for the participating province, and

          [D]{.dfn}
          :   is the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the
              person may reasonably be regarded as holding or investing
              funds for the benefit of persons that are resident in the
              participating province,
          :::
      :::

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the tax is payable under section 212.1 or
      218.1 or subsection 220.06(1) of the Act in respect of tangible
      personal property, the amount determined by the formula

      ::: {#758467 .Subparagraph}
      E -- F

      where

      [E]{.dfn}
      :   is the amount of that tax, and

      [F]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is determined for a
          participating province by the formula
          ::: {#758471 .NestedFormula}
          G × H

          where

          [G]{.dfn}
          :   is the amount of tax that would have become payable under
              that section or subsection in respect of the property at
              the particular time if that tax were calculated at the tax
              rate for the participating province, and

          [H]{.dfn}
          :   is the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the
              person may reasonably be regarded as holding or investing
              funds for the benefit of persons that are resident in the
              participating province,
          :::
      :::

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} where the tax is payable under section 218.1 or
      subsection 220.08(1) of the Act in respect of a supply of
      intangible personal property or a service on an amount of
      consideration for the supply, the amount determined by the formula

      ::: {#758476 .Subparagraph}
      I -- J

      where

      [I]{.dfn}
      :   is the amount of that tax, and

      [J]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is determined for a
          participating province by the formula
          ::: {#758480 .NestedFormula}
          K × L

          where

          [K]{.dfn}
          :   is the amount of tax that would have become payable under
              that section or subsection in respect of the supply at the
              particular time if the supply were acquired by the person
              for consumption, use or supply exclusively in the
              participating province, and

          [L]{.dfn}
          :   is the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the
              person may reasonably be regarded as holding or investing
              funds for the benefit of persons that are resident in the
              participating province, and
          :::
      :::

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if the tax is payable under subsection 220.05(1)
      or 220.07(1) of the Act in respect of bringing tangible personal
      property into a particular participating province, the amount
      determined by the formula

      ::: {#758485 .Subparagraph}
      M -- N

      where

      [M]{.dfn}
      :   is the amount of that tax, and

      [N]{.dfn}
      :   is the total of all amounts, each of which is determined for a
          participating province by the formula
          ::: {#758489 .NestedFormula}
          O × P

          where

          [O]{.dfn}
          :   is the amount of tax that would have become payable under
              that subsection in respect of the bringing in of the
              property if the property were brought into the
              participating province, and

          [P]{.dfn}
          :   is the extent (expressed as a percentage) to which the
              person may reasonably be regarded as holding or investing
              funds for the benefit of persons that are resident in the
              participating province.
          :::
      :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed amount --- subsection
  263.01(4) of Act

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 263.01(4) of the Act,
  an amount of tax that becomes payable by a person referred to in
  subsection (1), or that is paid by the person without having become
  payable, in respect of a supply that is acquired in whole or in part
  for consumption, use or supply in the course of activities related to
  a provincial series of the person is a prescribed amount of tax.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restrictions on rebates

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 261.4(d) of the Act, the following are prescribed
circumstances:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a rebate under section 261.1 or 261.3
  of the Act, the rebate is substantiated by a receipt that includes tax
  of at least \$5 and the person is otherwise eligible for a rebate
  under that section of that tax; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total of all amounts, each of which is an amount
  of a rebate for which the person is otherwise eligible under any of
  sections 261.1 to 261.31 of the Act and in respect of which the rebate
  application is made, is at least \$25.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-71, s. 11
:::

Version](section-22-20100701.html "Link to previous version of section 22"){.PITLink}
