### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Non-taxable Property and Services]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758503 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional specified item

**[[22.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this Division, property in respect of which a person has been paid or
credited, or is entitled to be paid or credited, a qualifying amount (as
defined in section 1 of the [Credit for Provincial Relief (HST)
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2011-57)) is a specified item in
respect of Ontario.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2011-56, s. 30
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-taxable property

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 23 of Part I of Schedule X to the Act, the following property is
prescribed:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} property that would be non-taxable property under
  section 6 of Part I of Schedule X to the Act if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the reference to "non-participating province" were
    read as a reference to "participating province",

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the reference to "a participating province" were
    read as a reference to "another participating province",

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the reference to "who is not resident in the
    participating provinces" were read as a reference to "who is
    resident in a participating province", and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the reference to "that participating province"
    were read as a reference to "the particular participating province";

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} property that is given as a gift or bequest to an
  individual who is resident in a participating province and that is
  brought into a participating province, if the property is

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} personal and household effects of an individual who
    died in a participating province and was, at the time of death,
    resident in a participating province, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} personal and household effects received, by an
    individual who is resident in a participating province, as a result
    or in anticipation of the death of an individual who was resident in
    a participating province;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} medals, trophies and other prizes, not including
  usual merchantable goods, that are won in a participating province in
  competitions, that are bestowed, received or accepted in a
  participating province or that are donated by persons in a
  participating province, for heroic deeds, valour or distinction;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} property that is brought into a participating
  province at a particular time by a person after having been acquired
  in, or brought into, a particular participating province by the person
  at another time in circumstances in which tax was payable by the
  person under subsection 165(2) or 218.1(1) of the Act or Division IV.1
  of Part IX of the Act if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the rate at which that tax was calculated is
    greater than, or equal to, the tax rate for the participating
    province at the particular time,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person was not entitled to a rebate of any
    part of that tax under section 261.1 of the Act, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the property was not a specified item in respect
    of the particular participating province at the other time;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} property that a person brings at any time into a
  participating province and that at that time is being supplied in a
  participating province to the person by way of lease, licence or
  similar arrangement under which continuous possession or use of the
  property is provided for a period of more than three months and in
  circumstances in which tax under subsection 165(1) of the Act is
  payable by the person in respect of that supply;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} property that is brought into a participating
  province at a particular time by a person after having been imported
  by the person at another time in circumstances in which tax was
  imposed under subsection 212.1(2) of the Act at the tax rate for a
  particular participating province if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the rate at which that tax was calculated is
    greater than, or equal to, the tax rate for the participating
    province at the particular time,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person was not entitled to a rebate of any
    part of that tax under section 261.2 of the Act, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the property was not a specified item in respect
    of the particular participating province at the other time;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} property that is brought into a participating
  province at a particular time by a person after having been used in
  and, at another time, removed from any participating province (in this
  paragraph referred to as the "specified province") by the person if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the tax rate for the specified province at the
    other time is greater than, or equal to, the tax rate for the
    participating province at the particular time,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where tax was payable by the person under
    subsection 165(2) of the Act in respect of a supply of the property
    to the person made in the specified province, the person was not
    entitled to a rebate of any part of that tax under section 261.1 of
    the Act, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the property was not a specified item in respect
    of the specified province at the other time; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} a specified motor vehicle that is brought into a
  participating province by a person after having been supplied to the
  person by way of sale in a participating province in circumstances in
  which tax was not payable under subsection 165(1) of the Act in
  respect of the supply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-191, s. 26(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20100701.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-taxable property and services

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 7 of Part II of Schedule X to the Act, a supply of a service
(other than a custodial or nominee service in respect of securities or
precious metals of the recipient of the supply) that is in respect of
tangible personal property and that is acquired by the recipient of the
supply in any circumstances is a prescribed supply if the tangible
personal property is removed from a particular participating province to
another participating province as soon after the service is performed as
is reasonable having regard to the circumstances surrounding the removal
and is not consumed, used or supplied in the particular participating
province after the service is performed and before the removal of the
property.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-taxable property and services

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 7 of Part II of Schedule X to the Act, a supply of a service
rendered in connection with criminal, civil or administrative litigation
(other than a service rendered before the commencement of such
litigation) that is under the jurisdiction of a court or other tribunal
established under the laws of a province, or that is in the nature of an
appeal from a decision of a court or other tribunal established under
the laws of a province, and that is acquired by the recipient of the
supply in any circumstances is a prescribed supply.

## [PART 6]{.HLabel1}[Recapture of Specified Provincial Input Tax Credits]{.HTitleText1} {#h-758539 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758540 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** The following definitions
apply in this Part.

[[British Columbia recapture amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[British Columbia recapture amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    specified provincial input tax credit in respect of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition in British Columbia of property;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the bringing into British Columbia of property;
      or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition of a service for consumption or
      use in British Columbia. ([montant de récupération de la
      Colombie-Britannique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eligible roadway]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eligible roadway]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a road, highway,
    bridge, tunnel or ferry landing that is for the passage of vehicles,
    but does not include a dedicated parking area, an airport runway, a
    shipyard, a driveway or a bicycle or pedestrian pathway. ([voie
    admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[farming]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[farming]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by
    subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([agriculture]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[industry classification system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[industry classification system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    North American Industry Classification System as it read on July
    1, 2010.  ([système de classification des
    industries]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[instalment period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[instalment period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any period beginning
    on the first day of the fourth fiscal month of a fiscal year of a
    person and ending on the last day of the third fiscal month of the
    immediately following fiscal year of the person.  ([période
    d'acomptes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[motor vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[motor vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a motorized vehicle
    designed for the transportation of individuals or of tangible
    personal property, but does not include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a power-assisted bicycle;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a snow vehicle;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an all-terrain vehicle;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an electrically propelled wheelchair;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a street car;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a vehicle that runs only on rails; or

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a farm tractor, or other farm machinery,
      acquired, or brought into a province, exclusively for use in
      farming activities. ([véhicule automobile]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[Ontario recapture amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Ontario recapture amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a specified
    provincial input tax credit in respect of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition in Ontario of property;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the bringing into Ontario of property; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition of a service for consumption or
      use in Ontario. ([montant de récupération de
      l'Ontario]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Prince Edward Island recapture amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Prince Edward Island recapture amount]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
    a specified provincial input tax credit in respect of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition in Prince Edward Island of
      property;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the bringing into Prince Edward Island of
      property; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition of a service for consumption or
      use in Prince Edward Island. ([montant de récupération de
      l'Île-du-Prince-Édouard]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[production]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[production]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an activity (other than the
    assembling, processing or manufacturing of tangible personal
    property in a retail establishment or the storage of finished
    products) that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the assembling, processing or manufacturing of
      particular tangible personal property to create other tangible
      personal property that is different in nature or character from
      the particular tangible personal property;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the generation of any form of energy or its
      transformation into another form of energy;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the restoring of tangible personal property by
      its owner;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the recording of images or sound on media;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the cutting, transforming and handling of timber
      in a forest and the building and maintenance of forest access
      roads in the course of those activities;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the extraction and processing of a mineral until
      the first stage of concentration or the equivalent;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the transformation of toxic industrial waste into
      a non-toxic product; and

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if performed in conjunction with an activity
      referred to in any of paragraphs (a) to (g) by the same person
      that performed that activity,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the detection, measurement, treatment,
        reduction or elimination of water, soil or air pollutants that
        are attributable to producing personal property,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the transportation of refuse or waste derived
        from producing personal property,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the quality control of personal property
        being produced or of production equipment, and

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the cleaning, screening, sifting, wrapping,
        packing or putting into containers of
        property. ([production]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[production equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[production equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} machinery, tools, equipment and accessories;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} moulds and dies;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} media for recording images or sounds;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} plans, drawings, models and prototypes;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} components or spare parts of the property
      described in paragraphs (a) to (d);

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} material used to produce or repair the property
      described in paragraphs (a) to (e); and

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} explosives and the material used to manufacture
      them. ([matériel de production]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[qualifying motor vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[qualifying motor vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a motor
    vehicle acquired in, or brought into, a specified province that is
    licensed, or required to be licensed, for use on a public highway
    under the laws of the specified province relating to the licensing
    of motor vehicles and that weighs, while carrying its maximum
    capacity of fuel, lubricant and coolant, less than 3,000 kilograms
    at the time when the vehicle is first licensed, or first required to
    be licensed, under those laws. ([véhicule automobile
    admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[recapture period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[recapture period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any period of 12
    calendar months that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} begins on July 1 of a calendar year; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} occurs between July 1, 2010 and June 30,
      2021. ([période de récupération]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[recapture rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[recapture rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} applicable at a time in
    respect of a specified provincial input tax credit means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a specified provincial input tax
      credit that is a British Columbia recapture amount,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after July
        1, 2010 and before April 1, 2013, 100%, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after
        April 1, 2013, 0%;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a specified provincial input tax
      credit that is an Ontario recapture amount,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after July
        1, 2010 and before July 1, 2015, 100%,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after July
        1, 2015 and before July 1, 2016, 75%,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after
        July 1, 2016 and before July 1, 2017, 50%,

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after July
        1, 2017 and before July 1, 2018, 25%, and

      - [(v)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after July
        1, 2018, 0%; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a specified provincial input tax
      credit that is a Prince Edward Island recapture amount,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after April
        1, 2013 and before April 1, 2018, 100%,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after
        April 1, 2018 and before April 1, 2019, 75%,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after
        April 1, 2019 and before April 1, 2020, 50%,

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after
        April 1, 2020 and before April 1, 2021, 25%, and

      - [(v)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a time that is on or after April
        1, 2021, 0%. ([taux de récupération]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

[[specified energy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specified energy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} electricity, gas and steam; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} anything (other than fuel for use in a propulsion
      engine) that can be used to generate energy

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} by way of combustion or oxidization, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} by undergoing a nuclear reaction in a reactor
        for the generation of energy. ([forme d'énergie
        déterminée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[specified province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specified province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means Ontario, British
    Columbia or Prince Edward Island. ([province
    déterminée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[specified time]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specified time]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means in respect of a
    specified provincial input tax credit of a person that is
    attributable to tax in respect of a supply, importation or bringing
    into a province of a specified property or service that becomes
    payable by the person, that is paid by the person without having
    become payable or that would have become payable by the person if
    the rules described in paragraphs 29(1)(a) to (g) applied in respect
    of that supply, importation or bringing in or if subsection 29(2)
    applied in respect of that bringing in,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case where that tax would have only become
      payable by the person if the rules described in paragraphs
      29(1)(a) to (g) applied in respect of that supply, importation or
      bringing in or if subsection 29(2) applied in respect of that
      bringing in, the day on which that tax would have become payable
      by the person; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the earlier of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which that tax becomes payable by
        the person, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the day that is the later of July 1, 2010 and
        the day on which that tax is paid by the person. ([moment
        déterminé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-191, s. 27
- SOR/2013-44, s. 28
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Prescribed Persons]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758621 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Large business

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of the definition [[large business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  in subsection 236.01(1) of the Act, a person is a prescribed person
  for a recapture period if the person is a registrant whose recapture
  input tax credit threshold amount in respect of the recapture period
  exceeds \$10,000,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Large business

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the definition [[large
  business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 236.01(1) of the Act, a
  person is a prescribed person at any time if, at that time, the person
  is a registrant that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a bank;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a corporation licensed or otherwise authorized
    under the laws of Canada or of a province to carry on in Canada the
    business of offering to the public its services as a trustee;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a credit union;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an insurer or any other person whose principal
    business is providing insurance under insurance policies;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a segregated fund of an insurer;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} an investment plan (as defined in subsection 149(5)
    of the Act);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation; or

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a person related to a person described in any of
    paragraphs (a) to (f).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recapture input tax credit threshold
  amount

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the recapture input
  tax credit threshold amount of a particular person in respect of a
  recapture period is equal to the total of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount determined by the formula

    ::: {#758638 .Paragraph}
    A × (365/B)

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the total of all consideration (other than consideration
        referred to in section 167.1 of the Act that is attributable to
        goodwill of a business) that became due to the particular person
        in the last fiscal year (in this subsection referred to as the
        "base year") of the particular person ending before the first
        day of the recapture period, or that was paid to the particular
        person in the base year without having become due, for taxable
        supplies (other than supplies of financial services and supplies
        by way of sale of real property that is capital property of the
        particular person) made in Canada, or made outside Canada
        through a permanent establishment in Canada, by the particular
        person, and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of days in the base year,
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total of all amounts, each of which is
    determined, in respect of a person (in this paragraph referred to as
    the "associate") that is associated with the particular person at
    the end of the base year, by the formula

    ::: {#758643 .Paragraph}
    C × (365/D)

    where

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the total of all consideration (other than consideration
        referred to in section 167.1 of the Act that is attributable to
        goodwill of a business) that became due to the associate in the
        last fiscal year of the associate ending before the first day of
        the recapture period, or that was paid to the associate in that
        fiscal year without having become due, for taxable supplies
        (other than supplies of financial services and supplies by way
        of sale of real property that is capital property of the
        associate) made in Canada, or made outside Canada through a
        permanent establishment in Canada, by the associate, and

    [D]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of days in the last fiscal year of the associate
        ending before the first day of the recapture period, and
    :::

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the total of all amounts, each of which is
    determined, in respect of a person (in this paragraph referred to as
    the "vendor") that makes a supply of a business to the particular
    person if, under the agreement for the supply, the particular person
    acquires, at any time during the 12-month period immediately
    preceding the recapture period, ownership, possession or use of all
    or substantially all of the property that can reasonably be regarded
    as being necessary for the particular person to be capable of
    carrying on the business and the vendor would, in the absence of
    paragraph (b), have a recapture input tax credit threshold amount
    exceeding \$10,000,000 in respect of the particular recapture period
    that includes that time, by the formula

    ::: {#758648 .Paragraph}
    (E/F) × (365 - G)

    where

    [E]{.dfn}
    :   is the total of all consideration (other than consideration
        referred to in section 167.1 of the Act that is attributable to
        goodwill of a business) for taxable supplies (other than
        supplies of financial services and supplies by way of sale of
        real property that is capital property of the particular person)
        made in Canada, or made outside Canada through a permanent
        establishment in Canada, by the particular person that became
        due to the particular person during the 12-month period
        immediately preceding the recapture period or that was paid to
        the particular person in that 12-month period without having
        become due, in relation to the business acquired by the
        particular person,

    [F]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of days in the 12-month period immediately
        preceding the recapture period that are after that time, and

    [G]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of days in the 12-month period immediately
        preceding the recapture period that are after that time and that
        are in the base year.
    :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3), consideration for
  a supply of property or a service includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any amount that would, in the absence of subsection
    153(4) of the Act, be included in the consideration for the supply;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any amount that would, in the absence of subsection
    156(2) of the Act, be consideration for the supply; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the supply is made between persons not dealing
    at arm's length, any amount by which the consideration is less than
    the fair market value of the property or service at the time the
    supply is made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Large business --- partnership

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a member (other than an individual) of a
  partnership acquires property or a service, or brings it into a
  specified province, for consumption, use or supply in respect of the
  activities of the partnership but not on the account of the
  partnership and the partnership is a large business, for the purposes
  of the definition [[large business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection
  236.01(1) of the Act, the member is a prescribed person at any time in
  respect of that acquisition or bringing in if, at that time, the
  member is a registrant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Large business --- joint venture

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If an operator (within the meaning of subsection
  273(1) of the Act) that is a participant in a joint venture with a
  large business under an agreement referred to in that subsection
  acquires property or a service, or brings it into a specified
  province, under the agreement on behalf of the large business in the
  course of the activities for which the agreement was entered into and,
  at the time of the acquisition or bringing in, an election under
  subsection 273(1) of the Act made by the operator and the large
  business is in effect, for the purposes of the definition [[large
  business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 236.01(1) of the Act, the
  operator is a prescribed person at any time in respect of that
  acquisition or bringing in.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Large business --- acquisition of
  control

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time in a recapture period, a person that
  is a large business acquires control of a corporation that is not a
  large business, the corporation and any persons associated with the
  corporation are, for the period beginning at that time and ending on
  the last day of the recapture period that includes the last day of the
  fiscal year of the corporation that includes that time, prescribed
  persons for the purposes of the definition [[large
  business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 236.01(1) of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Large business --- amalgamation

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time in a recapture period, two or more
  corporations (each of which is referred to in this subsection as a
  "predecessor") are merged or amalgamated to form one corporation (in
  this subsection referred to as the "new corporation") and the sum of
  the recapture input tax credit threshold amounts of the predecessors
  in respect of the recapture period exceeds \$10,000,000, the new
  corporation is, for the period beginning at that time and ending on
  the last day of the recapture period that includes the last day of the
  first fiscal year of the new corporation, a prescribed person for the
  purposes of the definition [[large business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
  subsection 236.01(1) of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Large business --- acquisition of a
  business

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} If a person makes, at any time in a recapture period,
  a supply of a business to another person that is not a large business,
  the person would, in the absence of paragraph (3)(b), have a recapture
  input tax credit threshold amount in respect of the recapture period
  exceeding \$10,000,000, the other person is acquiring, under the
  agreement for the supply, ownership, possession or use of all or
  substantially all of the property that can reasonably be regarded as
  being necessary for the other person to be capable of carrying on the
  business and, after that time, the business is carried on by the other
  person, for the purposes of the definition [[large
  business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 236.01(1) of the Act, the
  other person is a prescribed person for the period beginning on the
  earlier of the day that the other person begins to carry on the
  business and the day on which the other person acquires ownership,
  possession or use of all or substantially all of the property that can
  reasonably be regarded as being necessary for the other person to be
  capable of carrying on the business and ending on the last day of the
  recapture period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Large business --- becoming a
  registrant

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time in a recapture period, a person
  becomes a registrant and the recapture input tax credit threshold
  amount of the person or of another person that is associated with the
  person at that time exceeds \$10,000,000 in respect of the recapture
  period, for the purposes of the definition [[large
  business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 236.01(1) of the Act, the
  person is a prescribed person for the period beginning at that time
  and ending on the last day of the recapture period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ceasing to be a large business ---
  addition

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the definition [[large
  business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 236.01(1) of the Act, a
  person is a prescribed person at the time prescribed by section 30 or
  32 in respect of a specified provincial input tax credit of the person
  in respect of a specified property or service if the person ceased to
  be a large business

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} before that time; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a qualifying motor vehicle in
      respect of which section 32 applies, the time when the person
      first uses the qualifying motor vehicle otherwise than exclusively
      for the purpose referred to in subparagraph 28(2)(g)(i), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the specified time in respect
      of the specified provincial input tax credit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ceasing to be a large business ---
  deduction

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the definition [[large
  business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 236.01(1) of the Act, a
  person is a prescribed person at the time prescribed by section 33 in
  respect of a qualifying motor vehicle if the person ceased to be a
  large business

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} before that time; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after the end of the reporting period in which the
    person was required to add an amount to its net tax for the
    reporting period as a consequence of subsection 31(2) in respect of
    a specified provincial input tax credit of the person in respect of
    the qualifying motor vehicle.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Large business --- exclusion

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) to (10), for the purposes of
  the definition [[large business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection
  236.01(1) of the Act, a person is not a prescribed person at any time
  if, at that time, the person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a public service body;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2013-71, s. 12\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an entity of the government of Canada that is not
    listed in Schedule I of the Federal Provincial Fiscal Arrangements
    Act;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a department (as defined in section 2 of the
    [Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11)); or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an entity of the government of a province that is
    eligible, pursuant to a provision of a sales tax harmonization
    agreement with that province, for a rebate of tax paid under Part IX
    of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-191, s. 28
- SOR/2013-71, s. 12
:::

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Prescribed Property or Service]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758689 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified property or service

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of the definition [[specified property or
  service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 236.01(1) of the Act, the
  following property and services are prescribed:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a qualifying motor vehicle that is acquired in, or
    brought into, a specified province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} motive fuel, other than diesel fuel, that is
    acquired in, or brought into, a specified province for consumption
    or use in the engine of a qualifying motor vehicle;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} property (other than property for maintenance or
    repair) that is acquired in, or brought into, a specified province
    by a person in respect of a qualifying motor vehicle acquired in, or
    brought into, a specified province by the person if the acquisition
    or bringing in of the property occurs within 365 days of the
    acquisition or bringing in of the qualifying motor vehicle;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a service (other than a service for maintenance or
    repair) that is acquired for consumption or use in a specified
    province in respect of a qualifying motor vehicle acquired in, or
    brought into, a specified province by a person if the acquisition of
    the service occurs within 365 days of the acquisition or bringing in
    of the qualifying motor vehicle;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} specified energy that is acquired in, or brought
    into, a specified province other than qualifying heating oil, as
    defined in section 1 of the [Deduction for Provincial Rebate
    (GST/HST) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-65), acquired in,
    or brought into Prince Edward Island;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a service described in paragraph (a) of the
    definition [[telecommunication service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
    subsection 123(1) of the Act if the service is acquired for
    consumption or use in a specified province;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} access to a telecommunications circuit, line,
    frequency, channel or partial channel, or to other similar means of
    transmitting a telecommunication (but not including a satellite
    channel), for use in providing a service described in paragraph (a)
    of the definition [[telecommunication service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
    in subsection 123(1) of the Act if the access is acquired for
    consumption or use in a specified province; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} food, beverages or entertainment acquired in a
    specified province and in respect of which subsection 67.1(1) of the
    [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) applies or would apply if the
    person were a taxpayer under that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified property or service ---
  exclusion

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), for the purposes of the
  definition [[specified property or service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
  subsection 236.01(1) of the Act, property or a service referred to in
  any of paragraphs (1)(a) to (h) is not a prescribed property or
  service if the property or service is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specified energy acquired in, or brought into, a
    specified province for consumption or use exclusively in the heating
    of asphalt to be used directly in the construction or maintenance of
    an eligible roadway;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} property or a service described in any of
    paragraphs (1)(e) to (g) acquired in, or brought into, a specified
    province by the organizer or sponsor of a convention for consumption
    or use exclusively at the convention;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a 1-800, 1-866, 1-877 or 1-888 telephone service or
    a similar toll free telephone service or a service described in
    paragraph (1)(f) or (g) that is related to a 1-800, 1-866, 1-877 or
    1-888 telephone service or a similar toll free telephone service;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} access to the Internet;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a web-hosting service;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a taxi the operation and custody of which is
    entrusted to a person by the holder of a taxi permit for the taxi;
    or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} property or service acquired in, or brought into, a
    specified province exclusively for the purpose of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} being supplied by a person,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} becoming a component of tangible personal
      property that is to be supplied by a person, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of property or a service described
      in paragraph (1)(f) or (g) acquired by a person operating a
      telecommunication service, being used directly and solely in the
      making of a taxable supply of a telecommunication service by the
      person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-44, s. 29
:::

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Prescribed Amount, Conditions and Circumstances]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758715 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified provincial input tax credit

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph (b) of the definition [[specified provincial
  input tax credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 236.01(1) of the
  Act, a prescribed amount in respect of an amount that would be an
  input tax credit of a person in respect of a specified property or
  service attributable to tax under subsection 165(2) or section 212.1
  or 218.1 of the Act or Division IV.1 of Part IX of the Act is all of
  the amount that would be such an input tax credit if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the specified property or service
    is acquired, or brought into a specified province, by the person for
    consumption, use or supply exclusively in the course of commercial
    activities and, as a result of the consumption, use or supply
    exclusively in the course of commercial activities, no tax under
    section 218.1 of the Act or Division IV.1 of Part IX of the Act is
    payable in respect of the acquisition or bringing in, tax under that
    section or that Division had been payable in respect of the
    acquisition or bringing in;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a supply of the specified property
    or service deemed under subsection 143(1) of the Act to have been
    made outside Canada, the supply had not been deemed to have been
    made outside Canada;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a supply of the specified property
    or service that is deemed under Part IX of the Act to have been made
    for nil consideration, the supply had not been deemed to have been
    made for nil consideration;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a supply of the specified property
    or service that is deemed under paragraph 273(1)(c) of the Act not
    to be a supply, the supply had not been deemed not to be a supply;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the specified property or service
    is supplied to the person in a specified province or brought into a
    specified province by the person and is an item included in the
    Schedule to the [Deduction for Provincial Rebate (GST/HST)
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-65) applicable to the
    specified province in respect of which an amount may be paid or
    credited under an Act of the legislature of the specified province,
    the specified property or service were not an item included in that
    Schedule;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a supply of the specified property
    or service made between persons not dealing with each other at arm's
    length for no consideration, the supply had been made for
    consideration, paid at the time the supply was made, of a value
    equal to the fair market value of the property or service at that
    time; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a supply of the specified property
    or service made between persons not dealing with each other at arm's
    length for consideration less than the fair market value of the
    specified property or service at the time the supply is made, the
    value of the consideration were equal to the fair market value of
    the specified property or service at that time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified provincial input tax credit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time, a person brings tangible personal
  property into a specified province from a participating province and,
  as a consequence of that bringing in, the tangible personal property
  becomes a specified property or service at that time, for the purposes
  of paragraph (b) of the definition [[specified provincial input tax
  credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 236.01(1) of the Act, a
  prescribed amount in respect of an amount that would be an input tax
  credit of the person in respect of the tangible personal property
  attributable to tax under subsection 165(2) of the Act is equal to the
  amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#758728 .Subsection}
  A - B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount that would be such an input tax credit if
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a taxable supply (other than a zero rated
        supply) of the tangible personal property were made in the
        specified province to the person at that time,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the consideration for the supply were equal to
        the value, determined under the description of B in section 9,
        in respect of the tangible personal property at that time,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the tangible personal property were not an item
        included in the Schedule to the [Deduction for Provincial Rebate
        (GST/HST) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2001-65) applicable
        to the specified province in respect of which an amount may be
        paid or credited under an Act of the legislature of the
        specified province, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} tax in respect of the supply calculated on that
        consideration were paid by the person at that time; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount, if any, that is a specified provincial input tax
      credit of the person attributable to tax under Division IV.1 of
      Part IX of the Act in respect of that bringing in.
  :::

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2011-56, s. 31
:::

:::

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Addition to Net Tax --- Prescribed Time]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758738 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed time

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person acquires or imports a specified property or service or brings
  it into a specified province and tax under subsection 165(2) or
  section 212.1 or 218.1 of the Act or Division IV.1 of Part IX of the
  Act in respect of the supply, importation or bringing in becomes
  payable by the person, is paid by the person without having become
  payable or would have become payable by the person if the rules
  described in paragraphs 29(1)(a) to (g) applied in respect of that
  supply, importation or bringing in or if subsection 29(2) applied in
  respect of that bringing in, for the purposes of subsection 236.01(2)
  of the Act, the prescribed time in respect of a specified provincial
  input tax credit of the person in respect of the specified property or
  service is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the specified property or service is a
    qualifying motor vehicle the supply of which is made under a lease
    and the person is required, in accordance with subsection 235(1) of
    the Act, to add an amount representing all or part of that tax in
    determining the net tax for a reporting period of the person, the
    last day of the appropriate reporting period determined under
    subsection 235(2) of the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the specified property or service is food,
    beverages or entertainment and the person is required, in accordance
    with subsection 236(1) of the Act, to add an amount representing all
    or part of that tax in determining the net tax for a reporting
    period of the person, the last day of the appropriate reporting
    period determined under subsection 236(1.1) of the Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case where that tax would have only become
    payable by the person if the rules described in paragraphs 29(1)(a)
    to (g) applied in respect of that supply, importation or bringing in
    or if subsection 29(2) applied in respect of that bringing in, the
    day that is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the particular day on which that tax would have
      become payable by the person if

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the reporting period of the person is a fiscal
        year,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the reporting period of the person is a fiscal
        quarter and either

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the particular day is in a fiscal month that
          is not the last fiscal month of the fiscal quarter, or

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} the particular day is in the last fiscal
          month of the fiscal quarter and the person has added an amount
          to its net tax in respect of the specified provincial input
          tax credit for the reporting period of the person that
          includes the particular day, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the reporting period of the person is a fiscal
        month and the person has added an amount to its net tax in
        respect of the specified provincial input tax credit for the
        reporting period of the person that includes the particular day,
        and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} otherwise, the first day of the reporting period
      following the reporting period that includes the day on which that
      tax would have become payable by the person; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the earlier of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day that is

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the particular day on which that tax becomes
        payable by the person if

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} an input tax credit in respect of that tax is
          claimed in the return under Division V of Part IX of the Act
          filed for the reporting period that includes the particular
          day,

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} the reporting period of the person is a
          fiscal year,

        - [(III)]{.lawlabel} the reporting period of the person is a
          fiscal quarter and either

          [1]{.lawlabel} the particular day is in a fiscal month that is
          not the last fiscal month of the fiscal quarter, or

          [2]{.lawlabel} the particular day is in the last fiscal month
          of the fiscal quarter and the person has added an amount to
          its net tax in respect of the specified provincial input tax
          credit for the reporting period of the person that includes
          the particular day, or

        - [(IV)]{.lawlabel} the reporting period of the person is a
          fiscal month and the person has added an amount to its net tax
          in respect of the specified provincial input tax credit for
          the reporting period of the person that includes the
          particular day, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} otherwise, the first day of the reporting
        period following the reporting period that includes the day on
        which that tax becomes payable by the person, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the day that is the later of July 1, 2010 and

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the particular day on which that tax is paid by
        the person if

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} an input tax credit in respect of that tax is
          claimed in the return under Division V of Part IX of the Act
          filed for the reporting period that includes the particular
          day,

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} the reporting period of the person is a
          fiscal year,

        - [(III)]{.lawlabel} the reporting period of the person is a
          fiscal quarter and either

          [1]{.lawlabel} the particular day is in a fiscal month that is
          not the last fiscal month of the fiscal quarter, or

          [2]{.lawlabel} the particular day is in the last fiscal month
          of the fiscal quarter and the person has added an amount to
          its net tax in respect of the specified provincial input tax
          credit for the reporting period of the person that includes
          the particular day, or

        - [(IV)]{.lawlabel} the reporting period of the person is a
          fiscal month and the person has added an amount to its net tax
          in respect of the specified provincial input tax credit for
          the reporting period of the person that includes the
          particular day, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} otherwise, the first day of the reporting
        period following the reporting period that includes the day on
        which that tax is paid by the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed time --- Prince Edward
  Island

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In applying subsection (1) in respect of a specified
  provincial input tax credit that is a Prince Edward Island recapture
  amount, the reference to "July 1, 2010" in subparagraph (1)(d)(ii) is
  to be read as a reference to "April 1, 2013".

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-191, s. 29
- SOR/2013-44, s. 30
:::

### [DIVISION 6]{.HLabel2}[Addition to Net Tax --- Prescribed Manner]{.HTitleText2} {#h-758775 .Subheading}

#### [General Rules]{.HTitleText3} {#h-758776 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this section.

  [[selected person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[selected person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person that is not

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a financial institution;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a hotel, bar, coffee shop or restaurant;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an auto repair shop; or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a scrap metal dealer. ([personne
        désignée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specified production energy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specified production energy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the part
      of specified energy acquired in, or brought into, a specified
      province by a selected person for consumption or use by the
      selected person in the production of tangible personal property
      intended for sale or in the production of production equipment
      used to produce such tangible personal property, but does not
      include the part of the specified energy acquired in, or brought
      into, the specified province for consumption or use by the
      selected person in equipment for the air conditioning, lighting,
      heating or ventilating of the production premises or in other
      equipment if that consumption or use is not integral to that
      production. ([énergie déterminée pour la
      production]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specified research energy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specified research energy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of specified energy acquired in or
        brought into Ontario by a person, the part of the specified
        energy for consumption or use by the person in the course of
        activities in Ontario that are eligible scientific research and
        experimental development activities for the purposes of the
        Taxation Act, 2007, S.O. 2007, c. 11, Sch. A, and in respect of
        which the person deducts an amount in computing the person's tax
        payable under that Act; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of specified energy acquired in or
        brought into British Columbia by a person, the part of the
        specified energy for consumption or use by the person in the
        course of activities in British Columbia that are eligible
        scientific research and experimental development activities for
        the purposes of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), R.S.B.C.
        1996, c. 215, and in respect of which the person deducts an
        amount in computing the person's tax payable under that
        Act. ([énergie déterminée pour la recherche]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[specified salary and wages]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specified salary and wages]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of an employee
      means any salary, wages and other remuneration of the employee and
      any other amount that is, or is to be, included as income from an
      office or employment in computing the income of the employee for
      the purposes of the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([rémunération déterminée]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying motor vehicles and related
  property or services

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time during a reporting period of a
  person, the person is a large business and the particular time
  prescribed by section 30 in respect of a specified provincial input
  tax credit of the person in respect of a specified property or service
  described in any of paragraphs 28(1)(a) to (d) is in the reporting
  period, for the purposes of subsection 236.01(2) of the Act, the
  amount to be added to the net tax of the person for the reporting
  period in respect of the specified provincial input tax credit is
  determined by the formula

  ::: {#758792 .Subsection}
  A × B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the specified property or service is a
        qualifying motor vehicle in respect of which paragraph 30(1)(a)
        applies and the person is a large business at the time that
        would, in the absence of that paragraph, be the time prescribed
        by section 30 in respect of the specified provincial input tax
        credit, the specified provincial input tax credit,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the specified property or service is
        property or a service other than a qualifying motor vehicle in
        respect of which paragraph 30(1)(a) applies and the person is a
        large business at the particular time, the specified provincial
        input tax credit, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, zero; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the recapture rate applicable at the specified time in respect
      of the specified provincial input tax credit.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified energy

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time during a reporting period of a
  person, the person is a large business, the particular time prescribed
  by section 30 in respect of a specified provincial input tax credit of
  the person in respect of specified energy is in the reporting period
  and the person is a large business at the particular time, for the
  purposes of subsection 236.01(2) of the Act, the amount to be added to
  the net tax of the person for the reporting period in respect of the
  specified provincial input tax credit is determined by the formula

  ::: {#758801 .Subsection}
  A × B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if an election is made by the person under
        subsection (7) for the recapture period that includes the
        particular time and an election made by the person under
        subsection (8) is in effect at the particular time, the amount
        determined by the formula

        ::: {#758805 .NestedFormula}
        C × D × E

        where

        [C]{.dfn}
        :   is the specified provincial input tax credit,

        [D]{.dfn}
        :   is the percentage determined by the formula
            ::: {#758809 .NestedFormula}
            F/G

            where

            [F]{.dfn}
            :   is
                - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of specified energy
                  acquired in or brought into Ontario, the total of the
                  specified salary and wages of each employee of the
                  person that are paid by the person in the second last
                  taxation year of the person immediately preceding the
                  reporting period for anything done by the employee in
                  the course of, or in relation to, the office or
                  employment of the employee in Ontario to the extent
                  that it can reasonably be considered that those
                  specified salary and wages are not attributable to the
                  direct engagement by the employee in activities that
                  are eligible scientific research and experimental
                  development activities for the purposes of the
                  Taxation Act, 2007, S.O. 2007, c. 11, Sch. A, or

                - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of specified energy
                  acquired in or brought into British Columbia, the
                  total of the specified salary and wages of each
                  employee of the person that are paid by the person in
                  the second last taxation year of the person
                  immediately preceding the reporting period for
                  anything done by the employee in the course of, or in
                  relation to, the office or employment of the employee
                  in British Columbia to the extent that it can
                  reasonably be considered that those specified salary
                  and wages are not attributable to the direct
                  engagement by the employee in activities that are
                  eligible scientific research and experimental
                  development activities for the purposes of the [Income
                  Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), R.S.B.C. 1996, c. 215, and

            [G]{.dfn}
            :   is the total of the specified salary and wages of each
                employee of the person that are paid by the person in
                the second last taxation year of the person immediately
                preceding the reporting period for anything done by the
                employee in the course of, or in relation to, the office
                or employment of the employee in the specified province
                where the specified energy was acquired or brought into,
                and
            :::

        [E]{.dfn}
        :   is
            - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the specified provincial input tax
              credit is in respect of specified energy acquired in or
              brought into Ontario, the production carried on by the
              person in Canada during the last fiscal year of the person
              immediately preceding the reporting period is carried on
              primarily in Ontario and the most significant business
              activity of the person carried on in Canada in that fiscal
              year is described in the industry classification system
              under the code

              - [(A)]{.lawlabel} 113, 211, 212, 322, 324, 325, 327 or
                331, 4%,

              - [(B)]{.lawlabel} 311, 312, 313, 314, 321, 326 or 332,
                13%, or

              - [(C)]{.lawlabel} 315, 316, 323, 333, 334, 335, 336, 337
                or 339, 30%,

            - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the specified provincial input tax
              credit is in respect of specified energy acquired in or
              brought into British Columbia, of all the production
              carried on by the person in Canada during the last fiscal
              year of the person immediately preceding the reporting
              period, at least 10% is carried on in British Columbia and
              the most significant business activity of the person
              carried on in Canada in that fiscal year is described in
              the industry classification system under the code

              - [(A)]{.lawlabel} 113, 211, 212, 322, 324, 325, 327 or
                331, 4%,

              - [(B)]{.lawlabel} 311, 312, 313, 314, 321, 326 or 332,
                13%, or

              - [(C)]{.lawlabel} 315, 316, 323, 333, 334, 335, 336, 337
                or 339, 30%, and

            - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the percentage
              determined by the formula

              ::: {#758825 .NestedFormula}
              H/I

              where

              [H]{.dfn}
              :   is the amount that would be the specified provincial
                  input tax credit in respect of the specified energy if
                  the specified energy did not include specified
                  production energy and specified research energy, and

              [I]{.dfn}
              :   is the amount that would be the specified provincial
                  input tax credit in respect of the specified energy if
                  the specified energy did not include specified
                  research energy,
              :::
        :::

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if an election made by the person under
        subsection (8) is in effect at the particular time and no
        election is made by the person under subsection (7) for the
        recapture period that includes the particular time, the amount
        determined by the formula

        ::: {#758830 .NestedFormula}
        J × K

        where

        [J]{.dfn}
        :   is the amount that would be the specified provincial input
            tax credit in respect of the specified energy if the
            specified energy did not include specified research energy,
            and

        [K]{.dfn}
        :   is
            - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the specified provincial input tax
              credit is in respect of specified energy acquired in or
              brought into Ontario, the production carried on by the
              person in Canada during the last fiscal year of the person
              immediately preceding the reporting period is carried on
              primarily in Ontario and the most significant business
              activity of the person carried on in Canada in that fiscal
              year is described in the industry classification system
              under the code

              - [(A)]{.lawlabel} 113, 211, 212, 322, 324, 325, 327 or
                331, 4%,

              - [(B)]{.lawlabel} 311, 312, 313, 314, 321, 326 or 332,
                13%, or

              - [(C)]{.lawlabel} 315, 316, 323, 333, 334, 335, 336, 337
                or 339, 30%,

            - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the specified provincial input tax
              credit is in respect of specified energy acquired in or
              brought into British Columbia, of all the production
              carried on by the person in Canada during the last fiscal
              year of the person immediately preceding the reporting
              period, at least 10% is carried on in British Columbia and
              the most significant business activity of the person
              carried on in Canada in that fiscal year is described in
              the industry classification system under the code

              - [(A)]{.lawlabel} 113, 211, 212, 322, 324, 325, 327 or
                331, 4%,

              - [(B)]{.lawlabel} 311, 312, 313, 314, 321, 326 or 332,
                13%, or

              - [(C)]{.lawlabel} 315, 316, 323, 333, 334, 335, 336, 337
                or 339, 30%,

            - [(ii.1)]{.lawlabel} if the specified provincial input tax
              credit is in respect of specified energy acquired in or
              brought into Prince Edward Island, the production carried
              on by the person in Canada during the last fiscal year of
              the person immediately preceding the reporting period is
              carried on primarily in Prince Edward Island and the most
              significant business activity of the person carried on in
              Canada in that fiscal year is described in the industry
              classification system under the code

              - [(A)]{.lawlabel} 113, 211, 212, 322, 324, 325, 327 or
                331, 4%,

              - [(B)]{.lawlabel} 311, 312, 313, 314, 321, 326 or 332,
                13%, or

              - [(C)]{.lawlabel} 315, 316, 323, 333, 334, 335, 336, 337
                or 339, 30%, and

            - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the percentage
              determined by the formula

              ::: {#758847 .NestedFormula}
              L/M

              where

              [L]{.dfn}
              :   is the amount that would be the specified provincial
                  input tax credit in respect of the specified energy if
                  the specified energy did not include specified
                  production energy and specified research energy, and

              [M]{.dfn}
              :   is the amount that would be the specified provincial
                  input tax credit in respect of the specified energy if
                  the specified energy did not include specified
                  research energy,
              :::
        :::

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if an election is made by the person under
        subsection (7) for the recapture period that includes the
        particular time and no election by the person under
        subsection (8) is in effect at the particular time, the amount
        determined by the formula

        ::: {#758852 .NestedFormula}
        N × O

        where

        [N]{.dfn}
        :   is the amount that would be the specified provincial input
            tax credit in respect of the specified energy if the
            specified energy did not include specified production
            energy, and

        [O]{.dfn}
        :   is the percentage determined by the formula
            ::: {#758856 .NestedFormula}
            P/Q

            where

            [P]{.dfn}
            :   is
                - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of specified energy
                  acquired in or brought into Ontario, the total of the
                  specified salary and wages of each employee of the
                  person that are paid by the person in the second last
                  taxation year of the person immediately preceding the
                  reporting period for anything done by the employee in
                  the course of, or in relation to, the office or
                  employment of the employee in Ontario to the extent
                  that it can reasonably be considered that those
                  specified salary and wages are not attributable to the
                  direct engagement by the employee in activities that
                  are eligible scientific research and experimental
                  development activities for the purposes of the
                  Taxation Act, 2007, S.O. 2007, c. 11, Sch. A, or

                - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of specified energy
                  acquired in or brought into British Columbia, the
                  total of the specified salary and wages of each
                  employee of the person that are paid by the person in
                  the second last taxation year of the person
                  immediately preceding the reporting period for
                  anything done by the employee in the course of, or in
                  relation to, the office or employment of the employee
                  in British Columbia to the extent that it can
                  reasonably be considered that those specified salary
                  and wages are not attributable to the direct
                  engagement by the employee in activities that are
                  eligible scientific research and experimental
                  development activities for the purposes of the [Income
                  Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), R.S.B.C. 1996, c. 215, and

            [Q]{.dfn}
            :   is the total of the specified salary and wages of each
                employee of the person that are paid by the person in
                the second last taxation year of the person immediately
                preceding the reporting period for anything done by the
                employee in the course of, or in relation to, the office
                or employment of the employee in the specified province
                where the specified energy was acquired or brought into,
                and
            :::
        :::

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the amount that would be the
        specified provincial input tax credit in respect of the
        specified energy if the specified energy did not include
        specified production energy and specified research energy; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the recapture rate applicable at the specified time in respect
      of the specified provincial input tax credit.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Telecommunications

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time during a reporting period of a
  person, the person is a large business, the particular time prescribed
  by section 30 in respect of a specified provincial input tax credit of
  the person in respect of a specified property or service described in
  paragraph 28(1)(f) or (g) is in the reporting period and the person is
  a large business at the particular time, for the purposes of
  subsection 236.01(2) of the Act, the amount to be added to the net tax
  of the person for the reporting period in respect of the specified
  provincial input tax credit is determined by the formula

  ::: {#758866 .Subsection}
  A × B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is 100% of the specified provincial input tax credit, except that
      the person may, if the specified property or service is provided
      to the person together with other property or services that are
      not a specified property or service (each of which is in this
      subsection referred to as an "element") and the consideration for
      the specified property or service and each element is not
      separately identified, apply the following percentage
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the specified property or service is
        acquired for consumption or use in British Columbia, 95%, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the specified property or service is
        acquired for consumption or use in Ontario or Prince Edward
        Island and is provided to the person together with

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an element that is a service, 96%,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an element that is property, 89%, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an element described in subparagraph (i)
          and an element described in subparagraph (ii), 86%; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the recapture rate applicable at the specified time in respect
      of the specified provincial input tax credit.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Food, beverages and entertainment

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If, at any time during a reporting period of a
  person, the person is a large business and the time prescribed by
  section 30 in respect of a specified provincial input tax credit of
  the person in respect of a specified property or service described in
  paragraph 28(1)(h) is in the reporting period, for the purposes of
  subsection 236.01(2) of the Act, the amount to be added to the net tax
  of the person for the reporting period in respect of the specified
  provincial input tax credit is determined by the formula

  ::: {#758877 .Subsection}
  A × B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a large business at the time
        that would, in the absence of paragraph 30(1)(b), be the time
        prescribed by section 30 in respect of the specified provincial
        input tax credit, 50% of the specified provincial input tax
        credit, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, zero; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the recapture rate applicable at the specified time in respect
      of the specified provincial input tax credit.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Farmers

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (2) to (5), no amount is to be
  added to the net tax of a person for a reporting period of the person
  as a consequence of those subsections in respect of a specified
  provincial input tax credit of the person if the chief source of
  income for the taxation year of the person immediately preceding the
  reporting period is farming and all of the property or services to
  which the specified provincial input tax credit relates is primarily
  consumed or used in the person's farming activities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Selected listed financial institutions

  [(6.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (2) to (5), no amount is to be
  added to the net tax of a person for a reporting period of the person
  as a consequence of those subsections in respect of a specified
  provincial input tax credit of the person in respect of an amount of
  tax that becomes payable by the person while the person is a selected
  listed financial institution unless the amount of tax

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is deemed to have been paid by the person under
    subsection 171(1) or 171.1(2) of the Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is prescribed for the purposes of paragraph
    169(3)(c) of the Act or paragraph (a) of the description of F in
    subsection 225.2(2) of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election to use the SRED proxy

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3), a person may,
  before the first day of a recapture period, elect, for the recapture
  period, to determine the amount to be added under that subsection in
  respect of a specified provincial input tax credit (other than a
  Prince Edward Island recapture amount) of the person in respect of
  specified energy in accordance with a method described in that
  subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election to use the production proxy

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3), if a person
  produces tangible personal property for sale, the person may elect to
  determine the amount to be added under that subsection in respect of a
  specified provincial input tax credit of the person in respect of
  specified energy in accordance with a method described in that
  subsection at all times when the election is in effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form and manner of filing

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} An election made under subsection (8) by a person
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be made in prescribed form containing prescribed
    information;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} set out the day on which the election is to become
    effective, which shall be the first day of a recapture period;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be filed with the Minister on or before the day on
    or before which the person must file a return under Division V of
    Part IX of the Act for the reporting period of the person that
    includes the day on which the election is to become effective; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} remain in effect until a revocation of the election
    becomes effective.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} A person that has made an election under
  subsection (8) may revoke the election, effective on the first day of
  a recapture period that begins at least one year after the election
  became effective, by filing in prescribed manner with the Minister a
  notice of revocation in prescribed form containing prescribed
  information on or before the day on or before which the person must
  file a return under Division V of Part IX of the Act for the reporting
  period of the person that includes the day on which the revocation is
  to become effective.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-191, s. 30
- SOR/2013-44, s. 31
- SOR/2013-71, s. 13
:::
